Building on prior work that used Concept Activation Vectors (CAVs) to detect bias towards unwanted attributes (`concepts') in feature-based graders, we extend CAV-based analysis to two neural speaking assessment systems: a text-based BERT grader and a speech-and-text multimodal grader based on Whisper. CAVs represent human-interpretable concepts as directions in a model's activation space, allowing us to distinguish between whether a concept is encoded in a model's internal representations and whether it influences the predicted score, the latter quantified using a gradient-based sensitivity metric. Since CAVs rely on linear separability, which is less likely in complex neural embedding spaces, we also investigate whether sparse autoencoders (SAEs) provide cleaner concept directions by learning CAVs in a sparse latent space and mapping them back to activation space. Our analysis shows that concept recoverability depends strongly on the representation and architecture being probed, rather than on the concept alone. Sensitivity to concepts is also architecture-dependent. SAEs make concepts more linearly recoverable, but attenuate the original activation-space sensitivity, especially in low-dimensional layers. These findings highlight the need to distinguish concept recoverability from concept influence when auditing bias in speaking assessment systems.

</details>


### [97] [HarnessOpt-Bench: Evaluating LLMs at Harness Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06301)
*Varun Ursekar,Apaar Shanker,Yash Maurya,Shehab Yasser,Vijay S. Kalmath,Veronica Chatrath,Yuan Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HarnessOpt-Bench，一个用于衡量前沿LLM在端到端"harness优化"（即由AI系统迭代改进智能体外围代码）能力的基准。该基准在昂贵且随机的评估条件下运行，通过受信任的执行环境强制执行评估边界、计量目标智能体资源使用并保留候选版本供审计。实验对5个前沿LLM作为优化器进行了111次评分运行，结果显示优化器模型的差异大于其使用的编码harness，原生harness并不一致地更优，且增益在不同任务和种子情境下差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在智能体系统中的部署日益广泛，其能力不仅取决于模型权重，还取决于围绕它的harness（提示、工具、控制流、记忆和编排代码）。自动化harness优化——由AI系统进行迭代且评估引导的harness改进——既是提升AI系统的重要途径，也是对AI系统自身的一项高要求能力。然而社区缺乏衡量前沿LLM在此任务上表现的统一协议，因此需要建立这样的基准。

Method: 提出HarnessOpt-Bench基准：一个优化器（LLM配以编码harness）接收目标智能体的种子harness、分级评估反馈和固定的目标评估预算；它编辑harness并提名最终候选，以目标智能体在保留测试分区上相对种子的归一化增益进行评分。受信任执行环境强制执行评估边界、计量目标智能体资源使用并保留候选版本供审计。实验在4个下游任务上、在共享编码harness和原生harness两种条件下评估5个前沿LLM，共进行111次评分运行。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1) 优化器模型的区分度大于其所依赖的编码harness；(2) 原生harness并不一致地优于共享harness；(3) 增益在不同任务和种子情境下差异显著。这些结果确立了harness优化作为一项可测量、可区分的能力，且还有很大的提升空间。

Conclusion: Harness优化被证明是一个可测量且具有判别力的能力维度，当前前沿LLM在该任务上仍有显著改进空间。该基准为社区提供了一个统一协议，用于衡量和比较LLM在端到端harness优化方面的表现。

Abstract: As LLMs are increasingly deployed within agentic systems, their capabilities depend not only on the model weights but also on the harness: the prompts, tools, control flow, memory, and orchestration code surrounding them. This makes automated harness optimization -- the iterative and evaluation-guided improvement of a harness by an AI system -- both an important route to improving AI systems and a demanding capability for AI systems themselves. Yet the community lacks a common protocol for measuring how well frontier LLMs perform at this task. We introduce HarnessOpt-Bench, a benchmark for end-to-end harness optimization under expensive and stochastic evaluation. An optimizer, an LLM paired with a coding harness, receives a target agent's seed harness, graded evaluation feedback, and a fixed target-evaluation budget. It edits the harness and nominates a final candidate, which is scored by its normalized gain over the seed on a held-out test partition that remains inaccessible throughout search. A trusted execution environment enforces the evaluation boundary, meters target-agent resource use, and preserves candidate versions for audit. We evaluate 5 frontier LLMs as optimizers both under a shared coding harness and under their native harnesses across 4 downstream tasks, over 111 scored runs. Experiment results show that optimizer models separate more than the coding harnesses they act through, native harnesses are not consistently superior, and gains vary substantially across tasks and seed regimes. These results establish harness optimization as a measurable and discriminative capability with large space for improvement.

</details>


### [98] [Beyond Top-K: Replacing Black-Box Retrieval with Interpretable Agentic Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06305)
*Sagar Tamang,Ayush Vyas,Tabarakul Hazarika*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出READ（无嵌入的智能体文档检索）框架，针对金融、审计等长文档中表格密集的结构化检索问题，证明传统"分块-嵌入-检索"范式的结构性缺陷：在780页政府财务报告中86.8%的内容为表格行，数值继承表头的上下文常被分块边界切断导致量纲错误。READ通过确定性操作（规范化词汇检索、结构导航、受限跨度读取）经MCP协议暴露，将检索轨迹变为可重放的审计链，在51个验证问题上达到58.8%准确率，远超稠密检索的15.7%，但也诚实报告BM25与READ统计上无显著差异，说明增益来自"去嵌入化"而非"智能体迭代"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统对长文档采用统一的"切块-嵌入-取top-k近邻"设计，但对于财务报表、审计报告、监管报表这类结构性文档，该设计在根本上是不可靠的：大量相近数字在同一个嵌入空间中竞争，分块边界会切断数值与其单位（如lakh/crore）、财年表头之间的上下文关联，导致数量级错误。作者希望用可衡量的方式证明这一缺陷，并设计更可靠、可审计的替代方案。

Method: 首先通过实证统计（86.8%表格行、表头与数值行中位距离13行等）揭示分块范式在结构化文档上的结构性缺陷；再构建"钢人"（steelman）级别的表格感知分块器，证明其虽解决了单位问题但仍有27-30%的数值块缺少财年表头；最终提出READ框架——智能体通过三种确定性操作（规范化词汇检索、结构导航、受限跨度读取）经由Model Context Protocol直接读取原始文档，使每次检索轨迹成为可重放的审计轨迹而非不透明的相似度分数。同时设置消融实验：给同一循环配top-k工具以区分"接口增益"与"迭代增益"。

Result: 在51个人工验证问题上，READ达到58.8%准确率，显著优于稠密检索的15.7%（p_Holm=2×10^-5）；即使稠密检索经调优至35.3%，READ仍领先23.5个百分点（p_Holm=0.017）。消融实验显示，同一智能体循环若使用top-k工具仅达27.5%，表明增益主要来自接口设计而非迭代机制。但BM25与READ统计上无显著差异，说明结果分离的是"基于嵌入"与"无嵌入"的检索，而非"智能体"与"词法"检索。

Conclusion: 嵌入检索范式的弱点在于依赖相似度分数而非真实上下文结构，对表格密集文档尤甚。READ证明无需嵌入的确定性结构导航在结构化长文档检索上可以大幅超越稠密检索，但其增益属性应被精确刻画为"去嵌入化"而非"智能体化"。证据不支持BM25优于READ的结论，也不支持"智能体迭代本身带来提升"的说法。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation over long documents is dominated by one design: chunk the text, embed the chunks, and surface the top-k nearest neighbours of the query. We argue that for an important class of documents -- financial statements, audit reports, regulatory returns -- this design is structurally unsound, and we make the argument measurable. On a 780-page government financial report, 86.8% of content lines are table rows, thousands of near-identical figures compete in one embedding space, and a figure inherits its unit from a header a median of 13 lines above it -- so a chunk boundary routinely separates a number from whether it is in lakh or crore, an error of two orders of magnitude. A table-aware chunker built as a steelman fixes the unit problem but leaves 27-30% of numeric chunks with no fiscal-year header at every chunk size we tried. We propose READ (Reliable Embedding-free Agentic Document-search), in which an agent reads the raw document through three deterministic operations -- normalized lexical search, structural navigation, and bounded span reads -- exposed over the Model Context Protocol, so a trajectory is a replayable audit trail, not an opaque similarity score. On 51 verified questions READ answers 58.8% against dense retrieval's 15.7% (p_Holm = 2 x 10^-5) -- or 35.3% tuned, which READ still leads by 23.5 points (p_Holm = 0.017). An agent given the same loop but a top-k tool reaches only 27.5%, locating the gain in the interface rather than in iteration. We also report what the evidence does not support: BM25 is statistically indistinguishable from READ, so our result separates embedding-based from embedding-free retrieval, not agentic from lexical search.

</details>


### [99] [TRAJDEBUG: Tracing Error Lifecycle to Identify Critical Failures in Long-Horizon Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06346)
*Yunjia Qi,Zehua Yin,Xintong Shi,Hao Peng,Songyuanyi Lu,Yixian Liu,Richeng Xuan,Yuhong Liu,Zhichao Hu,Xiaozhi Wang,Lei Hou,Bin Xu,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TrajDebug，一个错误生命周期追踪框架，用于在LLM智能体失败轨迹中定位最早的关键错误步骤。通过多粒度历史压缩和基于证据的错误识别解决长轨迹问题，通过追踪每个错误的解决状态和终局影响实现关键归因。作者还构建了包含486条人工标注失败轨迹的基准TrajErrBench，实验表明TrajDebug优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体系统在复杂领域表现优异，但存在级联错误和调试困难的问题。关键错误检测旨在定位导致最终失败的轨迹中最早的错误步骤，但面临两大挑战：一是长轨迹中证据分散，难以识别单个错误；二是失败轨迹常包含多个局部错误，只有部分对最终失败负有责任。

Method: TrajDebug框架包含两个核心机制：(1) 长轨迹错误发现——采用多粒度历史压缩技术处理长轨迹中的证据分散问题，并结合基于证据的错误识别方法；(2) 关键归因——通过追踪每个错误的解析状态（resolution status）和终端影响（terminal impact）来确定哪些错误对最终失败承担关键责任。同时构建了TrajErrBench基准，包含486条来自Tau2Bench和SWE-Bench Pro的人工标注失败轨迹。

Result: 在多个智能体基准上的实验表明，TrajDebug在整体性能上优于现有基线。应用研究进一步证明，其诊断结果能为下游智能体提供可操作的反馈，提升任务成功率。

Conclusion: TrajDebug通过错误生命周期追踪的方式有效解决了长轨迹中关键错误检测与归因的难题，其诊断结果对下游任务具有实际改进价值。作者将发布代码和数据以促进进一步研究。

Abstract: LLM-based agentic systems have shown remarkable capabilities in complex domains, while suffering from cascading errors and difficulty in debugging. Critical error detection aims to locate the earliest error step in a failed trajectory that is responsible for the final failure. However, progress faces two main challenges. First, long trajectories make it difficult to identify individual errors, since the evidence for judging a step may be scattered across distant instructions, observations, and prior context. Second, failed trajectories often contain multiple local errors with different downstream effects, only some of which remain responsible for the final failure. In this work, we propose TrajDebug, an error-lifecycle tracing framework that addresses long-trajectory error discovery with multi-granularity history compression and evidence-based error identification, and supports critical attribution by tracing each error's resolution status and terminal impact. We further construct TrajErrBench, a benchmark of 486 manually annotated failed trajectories from Tau2Bench and SWE-Bench Pro, covering realistic tool-use and coding scenarios. Experiments across diverse agent benchmarks show that TrajDebug achieves the best overall performance over existing baselines, and application studies further demonstrate that its diagnoses provide actionable feedback for improving downstream agent success. We will release the codes and data to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [100] [Challenges in Evaluating Explanation Methods for Static and Evolving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06351)
*Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对可解释人工智能（XAI）评估不足的局限性，通过DetoxAI图像识别系统（用于偏见检测和概念遗忘）加以说明，并展示了图像分类解释方法的人类基础评估示例，进一步探索了适应概念漂移的演化数据流中解释的调整方法，讨论了反事实解释的适配经验，最后将其与数据、模型和解释协同演化的追踪挑战联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法存在评估不足的问题，且在实际应用（如空间领域）中，数据和模型不断演化，解释需要随之适应，但目前对解释在动态环境中的评估和适应方法研究不足。

Method: 通过DetoxAI系统展示XAI评估缺陷；采用人类基础评估（human-grounded evaluation）对图像分类解释方法进行评价；探索针对概念漂移数据流的解释适应方法，重点讨论反事实解释（counterfactuals）的适配经验。

Result: 通过具体案例展示了XAI评估不足的局限性，提出了人类基础评估的具体实践，并验证了反事实解释在概念漂移场景中的适配可行性。

Conclusion: XAI解释需要随数据流的演化而动态适应，数据、模型和解释三者协同演化的追踪是该领域面临的重大挑战，论文为此提供了初步的探索路径。

Abstract: This paper addresses the limitations of Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) with respect to insufficient evaluation. They are illustrated through the DetoxAI image recognition system for bias detection and concept unlearning. Then, an example of a human-grounded evaluation of methods for explaining image classification is presented. The paper further explores methods for adapting explanations to evolving data streams with concept drift. Experiences with adapting counterfactuals for this problem are discussed. Finally it is related to the challenges of tracking the co-evolution of data, models, and explanations.\footnote{This paper has been accepted for a publication in J.Nalepa (ed) Explainable AI in Space. Proceedings of EASi 2026 Workshop at IJCAI-ECAI 2026 Bremen, Springer CCIS vol 3107 (2016).}

</details>


### [101] [The Low Frequency Trap: Video Language Models Fail at Simple Event Bookkeeping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06361)
*Sarvesh Baskar,Zikui Cai,Shayan Shabihi,Anirudh Satheesh,Muhammad R. Islam,Udari Madhushani Sehwag,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"轨迹锚定的参数化分析"方法，用于视频事件计数的受控评估。通过在三个任务中生成2,190个视频，并配备可执行的轨迹真值，揭示了模型在事件计数上的阶段性时间失效模式：状态持续转换任务在低事件数下可靠，而瞬态闪烁事件始终不可靠；高计数高频率场景下最终计数正确率仅0.2%，事件恢复率仅18.1%。增加采样率虽有助提升最终分数，但报告序列与真值的一致性仍极低。该研究将视频评估从聚合准确率转向细粒度的时间推理诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有真实世界视频基准的固定片段无法解耦事件数量、频率、持续时间和视觉复杂度等因素，导致失败模式难以定位；程序化基准虽然可控，但仅对最终答案评分，无法对照可执行真值审计模型报告的事件。需要一个既能参数化控制又能进行逐事件级验证的评估框架，以精确定位时间推理失败的根源。

Method: 提出trace-grounded parametric profiling方法，在三个受控视频任务（弹球墙壁接触、视觉眨眼、类别状态转换）中系统变化事件计数N和频率F，同时保持渲染固定。共生成2,190个视频，每个视频配有可执行的事件轨迹，支持能力面估计和时间戳级别评估。通过80%可靠性阈值分析、采样率干预实验、提示策略对比及真实视频基准验证来全面诊断模型的时间推理能力。

Result: Gemini 3.6 Flash在状态持续转换任务中能可靠计数最多12个事件（0.5和1.0 Hz），但在瞬态眨眼事件上无可靠的正计数区域。高计数高频率场景下，最终计数正确率仅0.2%，事件恢复率18.1%。提高采样率使弹球任务准确率从19.6%提升至29.3%，但报告序列与真值的一致性仅3.7%。不同提示策略增益有限，真实视频评估同样显示模型仅在低事件数下表现可靠。

Conclusion: 事件表示形式决定了模型是否能够初始访问证据，该限制会随计数和频率的增加而加剧。额外的视频帧可能抬高最终分数却不产生忠实的事件恢复。轨迹锚定的参数化分析将视频评估从聚合准确率指标转变为对时间推理失败位置的详细诊断，为未来改进提供了精确的切入点。

Abstract: Real-world video benchmarks provide broad coverage, but their fixed clips entangle event count, rate, duration, and visual complexity, making failure modes hard to isolate. While existing programmatic benchmarks offer better control, they score only the final answer rather than auditing reported events against executable ground truth. To bridge this gap, we introduce trace-grounded parametric profiling for event counting in three controlled video tasks: bouncing-ball wall contacts, visual blinks, and categorical state transitions. Across 2,190 videos, we vary event count N and frequency F while holding rendering fixed. Each video includes an executable event trace for capability-surface estimation and timestamp-level evaluation. Our results reveal a staged temporal failure. At an 80% reliability threshold, Gemini 3.6 Flash reliably counts persistent state transitions up to 12 events at 0.5 and 1.0 Hz, yet demonstrates no reliable positive-count region for transient blinking events. Thus, event representation dictates whether a model initially accesses evidence -- a limitation that compounds as count and frequency increase. In the high-count, high-frequency regime, only 0.2% of final counts are correct and the model recovers just 18.1% of true events. To test if visual access is the primary bottleneck, we increase sampling rate. Although this boosts Bounce Ball accuracy from 19.6% to 29.3%, the reported sequence agrees with ground truth only 3.7% of the time. Extra frames can therefore inflate final scores without producing faithful event recovery. Different prompting strategies yield similarly limited gains, and real-world video evaluations show the same concentration of success at low event counts. Ultimately, trace-grounded profiling shifts video evaluation from aggregate accuracy metrics to a detailed diagnostic of where temporal reasoning fails.

</details>


### [102] [Tracing the Heart: An Evidence-Linked Pipeline for Heart-Failure Feature Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06366)
*Soorya Ram Shimgekar,Michelle Hu,Dorisa Shehi,Daniel Kang,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Koustuv Saha,Christian Poellabauer,Christopher Lee,Sajeev Singh,Piyum Zonooz,Navin Kumar,Zeeshan Ahmed,Priyadarshini Kachroo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Nimblemind多智能体系统（nMAS），一种基于证据链接和规则评分的自动化电子健康记录（EHR）特征工程流水线，专门用于心力衰竭领域。该系统在500条模拟患者记录、9个EHR源表的测试中，生成了132个结构化特征和70个规则评分聚合特征，将HFrEF表型分类的AUROC从0.895提升至0.963，HFpEF从0.870提升至0.910，并获得了81.5%的独立LLM证据支持与方法学评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHR特征工程是临床研究和AI中的主要瓶颈，占数据科学家39-45%的工作量，在心衰领域尤其突出。现有基于规则和大语言模型的方法只能提供部分自动化，缺乏可维护性和证据可追溯性。

Method: nMAS是一个证据链接、规则评分驱动的多智能体自动化特征工程流水线：使用500条从9个EHR源表构建的模拟患者记录，生成结构化特征和规则评分聚合特征，验证结构完整性、规则合规性和来源可追溯性，并通过受限LLM进行审计，最后以独立LLM进行规则评估。

Result: nMAS生成了132个结构化特征和70个规则评分聚合特征；加入聚合特征后，held-out AUROC在HFrEF表型分类中从0.895提升到0.963，HFpEF从0.870提升到0.910；独立LLM规则评估在证据支持和方法学合理性上获得最高分的81.5%。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了针对复杂心血管EHR数据实现自动化、可审计特征工程的可行性，但研究仅限单机构队列，需要外部验证才能确认泛化性。

Abstract: Electronic health record (EHR) feature engineering is a major bottleneck in clinical research and AI, accounting for 39-45% of data scientists' workload. This is especially pronounced in heart failure, which affects an estimated 6.7 million U.S. adults and requires integrating fragmented EHR data with disease-specific, guideline-based clinical reasoning. Existing rule-based and large language model (LLM)-based approaches offer only partial automation with limited maintainability and evidence traceability. We developed the Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS), an evidence-linked, rubric-grounded pipeline for automated heart-failure feature engineering, and evaluated it on 500 dummy patient records from nine EHR source tables. nMAS generated 132 structured and 70 rubric-scored aggregated features, verified for structural integrity, rubric compliance, and provenance, and audited by a restricted LLM. Adding the aggregated features improved held-out AUROC from 0.895 to 0.963 for HFrEF and 0.870 to 0.910 for HFpEF phenotyping, and an independent LLM-based rubric assessment of evidence support and methodological soundness scored the features at 81.5% of maximum points. These results demonstrate the feasibility of automated, auditable feature engineering for complex cardiovascular EHR data, though evaluation was limited to a single-institution cohort and external validation is needed.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Contour integral methods and model order reduction for parametric linear control systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05363)
*Serkan Gugercin,Mattia Manucci*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轮廓积分方法（CIM），结合基于投影的模型降阶（MOR）技术，高效计算参数化线性控制系统在指定时间区间内的输出，并通过贪心算法构建投影空间，达到用户指定精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化线性控制系统的输入输出关系计算在参数域上通常代价高昂，现有的投影式模型降阶方法在参数化场景下效率和精度之间存在权衡，需要更高效且可保证精度的方法。

Method: 采用轮廓积分方法近似拉普拉斯逆变换，沿修正积分轮廓施加求积规则；针对参数化系统，推导了误差估计，并基于该误差估计设计贪心算法构建投影空间；该投影框架天然满足Hermite插值条件，从而进一步降低成本。

Result: 在非参数化和参数化的基准控制系统中验证了方法的准确性和效率，与最先进的基于投影的模型降阶方法对比表现优异，且适用于多种输入函数及低维子空间可捕获的初始条件。

Conclusion: CIM与投影式MOR的结合能显著降低参数域上输入输出关系评估成本，同时通过Hermite插值条件和贪心误差估计保证精度，为参数化控制系统的仿真提供了一种高效可靠的方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces a contour integral method (CIM) for efficiently computing outputs of parametric linear systems in control form over specified time intervals and to a user-prescribed accuracy. The CIM approximates the inverse Laplace transform via a quadrature rule applied along a modified integration contour. For parametric systems, we show how CIM integrates effectively with projection-based model order reduction (MOR) where a greedy algorithm builds the projection spaces following an error estimate we derive for this setting. We additionally demonstrate that the developed projection framework naturally enforces Hermite interpolation conditions. This combination substantially lowers the cost of evaluating the input-output relations across the parameter domain, for a wide range of input functions, and for initial conditions well captured by a low-dimensional subspace.. We demonstrate the accuracy and efficiency of the approach on benchmark non-parametric and parametric control systems, comparing against state-of-the-art projection-based MOR methods.

</details>


### [104] [An Exact Solution of the Two-Ball Multi-Look Search Game with Three Boxes and Heterogeneous Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05406)
*Igor Kleiner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文精确求解了一个异构多查找搜索成本博弈：Hider将两个相同的球分布在三个盒子中（搜索成本满足a≥b≥c>0），Searcher自适应开盒直到找到两个球。博弈值等于三个显式有理函数的最大值。在三个参数区间内，最优Hider策略分别是限制在三个、五个或全部六个放置上的乘积形式分布。文章通过精确分析论证和贝叶斯（Bernstein）基证明给出了完整解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决异质多盒（multi-look）搜索成本博弈的精确解。此前类似问题（多目标搜索、多盒博弈）通常只能得到近似解或特定参数下的结果，本文旨在给出三个盒子、两个球情况下该博弈的完全封闭形式解，并验证所有混合策略在完整成本区域上的可行性。

Method: (1) 将所有非浪费的确定性Searcher策略等价化为72棵基本决策树，仅产生42种不同的开盒次数分布；(2) 通过解析论证解决第一个参数区间；(3) 使用两个精确的Searcher混合策略解决第二个区间，四个混合策略解决第三个区间；(4) 利用精确有理Bernstein基证书（certificates）验证参数化混合策略在整个成本区域上的可行性；(5) 通过独立的程序实现复现策略集、零和线性规划值及Bernstein验证器检查的所有154个二进节点。

Result: 博弈值被精确给出为三个显式有理函数的最大值。在三个参数区间中，最优Hider策略为乘积形式分布，分别限制于三个、五个或全部六个球放置方案。所有混合策略的可行性通过精确理性Bernstein基证书得到严格证明，且独立的实现完全复现了全部计算结果。

Conclusion: 本文给出了三盒双球异构搜索成本博弈的完整精确解。该结果不仅确定了博弈的精确值与最优策略结构，还通过Bernstein基证书方法提供了可验证的严格证明，为更一般的多盒多球搜索博弈研究奠定了方法基础。

Abstract: A Hider distributes two identical balls among three boxes whose search costs satisfy $a\ge b\ge c>0$. A Searcher opens boxes adaptively until both balls are found; every opening incurs the corresponding box cost and recovers at most one ball. We give an exact solution of this heterogeneous multi-look search-cost game. The value is the maximum of three explicit rational functions. In the three parameter regimes, an optimal Hider strategy is the product-form distribution restricted respectively to three, five, or all six placements. Every non-wasteful deterministic Searcher policy is payoff-equivalent to one of $72$ elementary decision trees, which induce only $42$ distinct opening-count profiles. An analytic argument settles the first regime. Two exact Searcher mixtures settle the second, and four settle the third. Feasibility of the parameterized mixtures over the full cost region is established by exact rational Bernstein-basis certificates. Independent implementations reproduce the policy set, the zero-sum linear-program values, and all $154$ dyadic nodes examined by the Bernstein verifier.

</details>


### [105] [A Relaxation-Based Decomposition Approach for Solving a Supported-Evacuation Problem in Wildfires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05413)
*Shahryar Moradi,Antoine Sauré,Jonathan Patrick*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对野火灾害中脆弱人群（如医院患者和长期护理居民）的疏散支持问题，提出了一种两阶段随机优化模型，同时优化设施选址、车队规模和车辆路径，并受严格时间窗约束。针对NP难复杂性，作者提出了基于逻辑型Benders分解的创新求解方法，包含组合型Benders割和逻辑型不等式。通过大量数值实验和科罗拉多州Roxborough Park社区野火演练的真实数据验证，该方法能产出高质量解，在避难所布置、车辆利用率及整体疏散效率上显著优于替代策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火灾害中脆弱人群（医院患者、长期护理居民等）的疏散支持问题尚未得到充分研究，亟需在严格时间窗下建立有效的设施选址、车队配置与路径规划的决策支持框架。

Method: 构建两阶段随机优化模型，联合优化设施选址、车队规模与车辆路径；针对问题的NP难特性，提出基于逻辑型Benders分解的求解方法，结合组合型Benders割与逻辑型不等式来加速收敛。

Result: 基于大规模数值实验及科罗拉多州Roxborough Park社区野火演练的真实数据，验证了所提方法能产生高质量解，并在避难所布局、车辆利用率和疏散效率方面显著优于现有替代策略。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段随机优化与逻辑型Benders分解方法能够有效提升野火场景下脆弱人群的疏散支持效率，为应急管理决策提供了可行的优化工具。

Abstract: This study addresses the critical yet under researched area of supported evacuation for vulnerable populations during wildfires, such as hospital patients and long term care residents, by developing a two stage stochastic optimization model that optimizes facility location, fleet sizing, and vehicle routing under strict time windows. To overcome the problem NP hard complexity, the authors propose an innovative solution methodology leveraging Logic Based Benders Decomposition, featuring Combinatorial Benders Cuts and logic based inequalities. Extensive numerical experiments and real world data from a community wildfire drill in Roxborough Park, Colorado, demonstrate that the proposed approach yields high quality solutions, significantly improving shelter placement, vehicle utilization, and overall evacuation efficiency compared to alternative policies.

</details>


### [106] [A proximal subgradient method for nonconvex stochastic optimization under the Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05460)
*Felipe Atenas,Alejandro Jofré,Pedro Pérez-Aros,David Torregrosa-Belén*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种近端随机次梯度方法，用于最小化非光滑非凸期望成本与下半连续、prox-bounded正则项之和。该方法采用Armijo型线搜索和逐步细化的样本平均近似，在仅需样本量非降无界的松弛条件下，建立了函数值和迭代点的几乎必然收敛性、全轨迹收敛性以及显式多项式收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机近端方法通常要求正则项的（弱）凸性或随机预言机方差的均匀有界性，且样本量的增长速率需满足特定预设要求，这些限制阻碍了方法在更一般非光滑非凸问题上的应用。

Method: 提出一种近端随机次梯度方法：每次迭代用逐步细化的样本平均替代期望成本，通过Armijo型线搜索选取近端次梯度步长，确保充分下降性质（至多包含样本近似引入的随机误差）。算法仅要求被积函数满足非光滑局部化的下降引理变体，该假设统一涵盖光滑Lipschitz梯度损失与凸函数之差。

Result: 在样本量序列仅需单调非降且无界的松弛条件下，建立了函数值序列的几乎必然收敛和所有聚点的平稳性；利用Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz性质将子序列收敛升级为整条轨迹收敛到单一平稳点；对指数型KL解奇异函数和多项式增长的样本量，导出函数值和迭代点的显式多项式收敛速率（至多差对数因子）。该收敛保证在光滑情形下也是新的。

Conclusion: 该框架比现有方法适用更广泛的问题设定，无需正则项的（弱）凸性或随机误差方差的均匀上界，收敛保证在光滑情形下也具有新颖性，为大规模非光滑非凸随机优化提供了更通用的理论保证。

Abstract: This work introduces a proximal stochastic subgradient method for minimizing the sum of an expected cost, whose integrand is potentially nonsmooth and nonconvex, and a lower semicontinuous, prox-bounded function. We target a broad class of integrands obeying a nonsmooth, localized variant of the descent lemma in the decision variable, a structural assumption that simultaneously covers smooth losses with Lipschitz gradient and differences of such losses with convex functions. At each iteration the expected cost is replaced by a sample average that is progressively refined, and the proximal-subgradient stepsize is selected by an Armijo-type line search enforcing a sufficient-decrease property up to stochastic errors induced by the sample-based approximation. This framework accommodates substantially more general problem formulations than existing methods, in particular, it requires neither (weak) convexity of the regularizer nor a uniform bound on the variance of the stochastic oracle, and our analysis yields convergence guarantees that are new even in the smooth setting. Specifically, we establish almost sure convergence of the sequence of function values and stationarity of every accumulation point of the trajectories under the relaxed requirement that the sample-size sequence be merely nondecreasing and unbounded, with no prescribed growth rate. Leveraging the Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz (KL) property, we further upgrade this subsequential guarantee to convergence of the whole trajectory to a single stationary point. Finally, for exponential-type KL desingularizing functions and polynomially growing sample sizes, we derive explicit polynomial convergence rates, up to a logarithmic factor, for both the function values and the iterates.

</details>


### [107] [An Inertial Block Proximal Linearized Method with Adaptive Momentum for Nonconvex and Nonsmooth Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05502)
*Weifeng Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向多块非凸非光滑优化问题的惯性块近端线性化方法（IBPL⁺-TP），该方法采用两阶段自适应动量策略和两个独立的、不受其他参数约束的外推点来加速收敛，并证明了目标函数单调收敛、序列全局收敛到临界点及其收敛率。在稀疏非负矩阵分解和稀疏非负CP分解两个机器学习问题上，该方法优于多种现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多块非凸非光滑优化问题广泛存在于震前异常分析和机器学习等应用中，现有方法在收敛速度和参数设置方面存在局限，需要更高效的求解算法。

Method: 提出惯性块近端线性化方法（IBPL⁺-TP），核心创新包括：(1) 两阶段自适应动量策略用于有效更新外推参数；(2) 允许使用两个不同的外推点加速收敛；(3) 两个外推点的外推参数彼此独立且不受其他参数约束。

Result: 证明了目标函数在该类问题上的单调收敛性，序列全局收敛到临界点，并建立了收敛率。在稀疏非负矩阵分解（ℓ₀约束）和稀疏非负CP分解（ℓ₀约束）两个非凸非光滑机器学习问题上的数值实验表明，该方法优于多种现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: IBPL⁺-TP方法在保持严格理论保证（单调收敛、全局收敛到临界点、收敛率）的同时，通过两阶段自适应动量和双外推点设计显著提升了实际求解性能，展示了在多块非凸非光滑优化问题上的优越性。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider a class of multiblock nonconvex nonsmooth optimization problems, which covers many applications such as the analysis of pre-earthquake anomalies and machine learning. To solve this class of problems, we propose the inertial block proximal linearized method with two-phase adaptive momentum (IBPL$^+$-TP). Compared to the current methods, our method possesses three main advantages: (1) it introduces a two-phase adaptive momentum strategy to effectively update the extrapolation parameters, (2) it allows using two different extrapolation points to accelerate the convergence, (3) it allows the extrapolation parameters of these two extrapolation points to be independent of and unconstrained by all other parameters. While maintaining the above advantages, we prove that our method ensures the monotonic convergence of the objective function of this class of problems, and we also prove that the sequence generated by our method globally converges to a critical point, as well as establish the convergence rate of our method. To demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, we apply it to solve two nonconvex and nonsmooth machine learning problems, namely sparse nonnegative matrix factorization with $\ell_0$-constraints and sparse nonnegative CP decomposition with $\ell_0$-constraints. The numerical experimental results on solving these problems show that our method outperforms several state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [108] [A Unified Framework for Iterate Convergence of Bregman Proximal Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05536)
*He Chen,Jiaming Fan,Anthony Man-Cho So*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的Bregman近端方法（BPM）迭代收敛框架，通过扩展尺度化Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz（SKŁ）性质，覆盖了广泛的核函数和复合目标函数，并首次给出了无需凸性假设或驻点孤立性假设的镜像流（连续时间BPM）轨迹收敛结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bregman近端方法（BPM）的迭代收敛问题长期以来悬而未决，尤其是对于非凸目标函数。已有工作（chen2026skl）仅针对Shannon熵核和线性约束问题建立了收敛性，缺乏统一的、适用更广泛核函数与目标函数的分析框架。

Method: 本文通过以下方式实现统一收敛框架：(1) 扩展chen2026skl中的分析工具，特别是SKŁ性质；(2) 引入依赖于核函数的参数化函数，证明扩展后的SKŁ性质对所有连续半解析函数（尤其当核具有闭域时）成立；(3) 验证标准BPM在温和正则性条件下满足框架假设；(4) 基于参数化函数进一步分析连续时间BPM（镜像流）在o-minimal可定义目标函数下的收敛性。

Result: 建立了统一的迭代收敛框架，适用于广泛的核函数和复合目标函数；证明了标准BPM在温和正则性条件下的迭代收敛；首次证明镜像流对o-minimal可定义目标函数收敛到驻点，且不需凸性假设或驻点孤立性假设。

Conclusion: 该工作为离散和连续时间的BPM提供了统一的轨迹收敛理论，填补了非凸BPM收敛性分析的空白，特别是镜像流收敛结果的首次建立具有重要理论意义。

Abstract: Iterate convergence of Bregman proximal methods (BPMs) has long remained open, especially for nonconvex objectives. Recently, \citet{chen2026skl} made progress by establishing iterate convergence for a BPM via the so-called scaled Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz (SKŁ) property, but only for the Shannon entropy kernel and linearly constrained problems. In this paper, we develop a unified iterate convergence framework that applies to a broad group of kernels and composite objective functions. Our approach extends the analytical tools in \cite{chen2026skl}, in particular the SKŁ property, which plays a central role in ensuring convergence of the generated sequences. By introducing kernel-dependent parameterization functions, we show that the extended SKŁ property holds for all continuous subanalytic functions, particularly when the kernel has a closed domain. We then verify that the assumptions of the framework are satisfied by standard BPMs under mild regularity conditions, thereby establishing their iterate convergence for a wide range of objective functions. Furthermore, based on the parameterization functions, we show that the continuous-time BPM (mirror flow) converges to a stationary point for o-minimal definable objective functions, yielding the first trajectory convergence result for mirror flow without imposing convexity assumptions on the objective function or isolation assumptions on stationary points. Taken together, these discrete- and continuous-time convergence results provide a unified trajectory convergence theory for BPMs.

</details>


### [109] [A Recentered-Domain Yau-Yau Filter for Target Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05559)
*Lei Ma,Yuzhong Hu,Xiaoming Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Recentered-Domain Yau-Yau Filter (RD-YYF)，通过将Yau-Yau滤波器中的Forward Kolmogorov方程(FKE)求解限制在以最新状态估计为中心的固定大小局部窗口内，并结合物理信息神经网络(PINN)离线生成解快照、主成分分析(PCA)降维及轻量残差代理模型实现在线快速推断。在几何约束目标跟踪实验中，RD-YYF相比EKF和PF具有更低的跟踪误差，同时保持高效的时间步推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yau-Yau滤波器将非线性状态估计转化为由Forward Kolmogorov方程(FKE)控制的概率密度传播问题，但将其应用于目标跟踪需要在有限计算域上高效近似FKE，这是一个关键挑战。现有方法在计算效率和近似精度之间权衡不足，需要一种既能保持高精度又能快速求解的方案。

Method: RD-YYF的核心方法包括：(1) 在固定大小局部窗口内求解FKE，窗口中心随最新状态估计动态重定(recenter)；(2) 离线阶段使用物理信息神经网络(PINN)生成FKE解快照，通过主成分分析(PCA)构建密度演化的低维表示；(3) 训练轻量残差代理模型，将初始条件系数和域中心映射到终端解系数；(4) 在线阶段利用预训练代理预测重定窗口内的密度演化，执行观测更新与状态估计。

Result: 在两个几何约束目标跟踪实例上的实验表明：RD-YYF的跟踪误差低于扩展卡尔曼滤波(EKF)和粒子滤波(PF)，同时保持了高效的单步推断。消融实验证实，域重定中心(recentering)机制改善了高概率区域的密度近似质量，并加速了离线PINN的收敛速度。

Conclusion: 实验结果展示了RD-YYF在高效非线性目标跟踪中的潜力，验证了域重定+数据驱动降维代理这一技术路线既能保证高精度又满足实时性要求，为Yau-Yau滤波器在实际目标跟踪中的落地应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The Yau-Yau filter reformulates nonlinear state estimation as probability-density propagation governed by the Forward Kolmogorov equation (FKE). Applying it to target tracking, however, requires efficient FKE approximation on a finite computational domain. This paper proposes a Recentered-Domain Yau-Yau Filter (RD-YYF), which solves the FKE within a fixed-size local window centered at the latest state estimate. This design concentrates numerical resolution near the dominant posterior density. Offline, physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) generate FKE solution snapshots, while principal component analysis constructs a low-dimensional representation of density evolution. A lightweight residual surrogate maps the initial-condition coefficients and domain center to the terminal-solution coefficients. Online, the pretrained surrogate predicts density evolution within the recentered window, followed by observation update and state estimation. Experiments on two geometrically constrained target-tracking examples show that RD-YYF achieves lower tracking errors than the extended Kalman filter (EKF) and particle filter (PF), while retaining efficient per-timestep inference. Ablation results indicate that domain recentering improves density approximation in high-probability regions and accelerates offline PINN convergence. These results demonstrate the potential of RD-YYF for efficient nonlinear target tracking

</details>


### [110] [SDDmiP.jl: A Software Package with a Provably Convergent Benders Algorithm for Multi-Stage Stochastic Mixed-Integer Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05567)
*Akul Bansal,Simge Küçükyavuz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个开源软件包，实现了多阶段随机整数规划问题的可证明收敛的Benders型分解算法。该算法除了标准割族（Benders割、强化Benders割、Lagrangian割）外，还引入了ReLU割以保证一般混合整数状态变量的收敛性。针对对偶问题多重最优解问题，提出了基于归一化和正则化的割选择策略，并引入了交替割准则，在四个多阶段随机整数规划问题类别上进行了计算实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段随机整数规划问题中，生成ReLU割的对偶问题往往存在多个最优解，虽然每个解都能产生有效割来分离当前解，但不同割对子问题成本的近似质量差异很大，因此需要有效的割选择策略来强化割的质量。

Method: 开发了一个开源软件包，实现了可证明收敛的Benders型分解算法。算法融合了标准割族（Benders、强化Benders、Lagrangian割）和ReLU割。针对对偶问题多重最优解，提出了基于归一化和正则化的两种割选择策略，并设计了交替割准则：在Benders割有效时使用更廉价的Benders割，仅在必要时调用更昂贵的紧割。

Result: 在四类多阶段随机整数规划问题上进行了计算实验，成功评估了所提出的割选择策略和交替割准则的性能，并揭示了问题结构对算法实际性能的影响。

Conclusion: 所提出的开源软件包及其割选择策略和交替割准则能够有效求解多阶段随机整数规划问题，且问题结构特征对割选择策略的实际效果有显著影响。

Abstract: We present an open-source software package that implements a provably convergent Benders-type decomposition algorithm for multistage stochastic integer programs. In addition to standard cut families, such as Benders, strengthened Benders, and Lagrangian cuts, the algorithm incorporates rectified linear unit (ReLU) cuts, which provide convergence guarantees for general mixed-integer state variables. However, the dual problems used to generate these cuts often admit multiple optimal solutions. Although each solution yields a valid cut that separates the incumbent, the resulting cuts can differ in how well they approximate the subproblem cost. To strengthen these cuts, our package implements and evaluates two cut-selection strategies based on normalization and regularization of the dual problem. We also incorporate an alternating-cut criterion that uses cheaper Benders cuts when they are effective and invokes more expensive tight cuts only when necessary. Computational experiments on four classes of multistage stochastic integer programs benchmark these methods and provide insights on how problem structure affects their practical performance.

</details>


### [111] [Convergence Rates for Variational Inequality Projection Neural Networks with a State-Dependent Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05574)
*Mohammed Alshahrani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究闭凸集上变分不等式的连续时间投影神经网络，针对一类既非Hessian生成也非标量度量的状态依赖矩阵度量，证明了投影的联合正则性、局部与全局指数收敛性，并发现强单调线性算子下存在周期轨道环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有收敛性分析覆盖两类度量：Hessian生成的逆度量（可用Bregman距离消去度量导数项）和状态依赖标量度量。本文旨在处理一种不属于以上任一类型的矩阵度量，填补该空白并建立相应的收敛性理论。

Method: (1) 证明投影在其自变量与度量两个参数上的联合正则性；(2) 在常见谱界条件下改进欧氏Lipschitz估计，从平方界改进到界本身；(3) 对Lipschitz强单调算子与Lipschitz度量证明局部指数收敛并给出显式收敛速率与半径；(4) 在紧可行性集上利用显式度量变化界建立全局指数收敛；(5) 分析强单调线性算子情形的周期轨道环，证明逆度量在整个环上违反Hessian可积性；(6) 通过确定性数值计算验证解析公式并量化收敛证书的松弛度。

Result: 证明了投影的联合正则性；欧氏Lipschitz估计从平方界改进为原始界；建立了局部和全局指数收敛性，含显式速率与半径；发现并刻画了周期轨道环，证明了逆度量在环上不满足Hessian可积性；数值实验验证了理论公式并量化了收敛证书中的松弛。

Conclusion: 本文扩展了投影神经网络的收敛性分析至更一般的矩阵度量，建立了完整的收敛性与动力学行为刻画，并通过数值验证确认了理论的正确性与界限的紧致程度。

Abstract: We study continuous-time projection neural networks for variational inequalities on closed convex sets. A positive definite matrix that depends on the state preconditions the operator, and its inverse defines the projection metric. Existing convergence analyses of this flow cover Hessian-generated inverse metrics and state-dependent scalar metrics. In the first case, a Bregman distance eliminates metric-derivative terms. We treat a matrix metric whose inverse is of neither kind. We prove joint regularity of the projection in its argument and metric. Under common spectral bounds, the Euclidean Lipschitz estimate improves from the squared bound to the bound itself. For Lipschitz strongly monotone operators and Lipschitz metrics, we prove local exponential convergence with explicit rate and radius. On compact feasible sets, an explicit metric-variation bound yields global exponential convergence. A twice continuously differentiable, uniformly positive definite metric and a strongly monotone linear operator produce an annulus of periodic orbits. The inverse metric violates Hessian integrability throughout the annulus. Deterministic computations confirm the analytic formulas and quantify slack in both convergence certificates.

</details>


### [112] [An inverse mixed-integer optimization framework for learning interpretable models of expert decision making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05585)
*Anurag Holani,Rishabh Gupta,John Wassick,Qi Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种逆优化方法，通过观察专家的决策来联合学习其偏好（感知成本）和决策规则，从而捕获和复现专家的决策过程。在班次分配、生产计划和真实路由三个案例中验证了方法的通用性和预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家决策过程往往隐含且难以明确表述，但理解并迁移这种知识对培训新手、提升人机系统性能以及实现全自主系统至关重要。因此需要通过观察专家的决策来学习可解释的决策模型。

Method: 采用逆优化（inverse optimization）方法，联合学习决策者的感知成本（偏好）和决策规则。该方法从专家的历史决策数据中反推目标函数中的成本参数以及约束条件中的决策规则，从而构建可解释的决策模型。

Result: 在三个案例研究（班次分配问题、生产计划问题、真实世界路由问题）中，联合建模感知成本和决策规则相比单独建模任一要素取得了更好的预测性能，表明所提出框架具有更大的灵活性。

Conclusion: 联合学习感知成本和决策规则的逆优化框架能够更准确、更灵活地捕获和复现专家决策行为，在复杂工程应用中具有广泛的适用性和实用价值。

Abstract: Understanding how experts make decisions and being able to transfer that knowledge is important, especially in complex engineering applications. It is highly valuable for training novices, improving the performance of human-machine systems, and potentially enabling fully autonomous systems that perform as well as human experts. However, an expert's decision-making strategy, developed through years of experience, is often not directly accessible, since the implicit preferences and decision rules involved can be difficult to specify explicitly. This has motivated the use of observed decisions made by the expert to learn an interpretable model that captures the expert's decision-making process. In this work, we develop an inverse optimization approach to jointly learn the decision-maker's preferences (or perceived costs) and the decision rules governing their choices. We demonstrate the general applicability of our approach using three case studies that consider a shift assignment problem, a production planning problem, and a real-world routing problem, respectively. Across these case studies, modeling both perceived costs and decision rules leads to better predictions, highlighting the value of the proposed framework and its greater flexibility in capturing and replicating expert decision making.

</details>


### [113] [Fast Gradient Algorithm with Dry-like Friction and Nonmonotone Line Search for Nonconvex Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05653)
*Lien T. Nguyen,Andrew Eberhard,Xinghuo Yu,Chaojie Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于Hilbert空间中可微（可能非凸）函数最小化的快速梯度算法。该算法将凸函数的干摩擦性质扩展至非凸情形的"类干摩擦"性质，并结合线搜索技术自适应更新参数。算法根据参数选择可实现对临界点的子序列收敛或对"近似"临界点的全序列收敛，并在Kurdyka–Lojasiewicz（KL）性质下建立全序列收敛。该算法可退化为多种现有的带Hessian阻尼和干摩擦的惯性梯度算法，并可通过Moreau包络处理弱凸非光滑优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的惯性梯度算法结合Hessian阻尼和干摩擦在凸优化中已取得良好效果，但将其推广到非凸设定面临挑战。此外，参数的自适应调整和收敛保证在非凸情形下尚不完善，需要统一的框架来涵盖并推广已有的多种算法。

Method: 1) 将凸函数的干摩擦性质扩展到非凸情形的"类干摩擦"性质；2) 采用线搜索技术在每步迭代中自适应更新参数；3) 利用Moreau包络的变分性质将算法适配到弱凸非光滑优化问题；4) 将凸KL函数的Moreau包络KL指数结果推广到更广泛的非凸且不一定连续的KL函数类。

Result: 该算法在不同参数选择下可实现子序列收敛到临界点或全序列收敛到近似临界点；在KL性质下可建立全序列收敛到临界点；通过灵活的参数选择，算法可退化出多种现有的惯性梯度算法；数值实验结果验证了算法的高效性及类干摩擦与外推、线搜索技术结合的优势。

Conclusion: 本文提出的快速梯度算法为非凸优化提供了统一的收敛性分析框架，兼具理论保证和计算效率，且能推广至弱凸非光滑问题，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a fast gradient algorithm for the problem of minimizing a differentiable (possibly nonconvex) function in Hilbert spaces. We first extend the dry friction property for convex functions to what we call the dry-like friction property in a nonconvex setting, and then employ a line search technique to adaptively update parameters at each iteration. Depending on the choice of parameters, the proposed algorithm exhibits subsequential convergence to a critical point or full sequential convergence to an ``approximate'' critical point of the objective function. We also establish the full sequential convergence to a critical point under the Kurdyka--Lojasiewicz (KL) property of a merit function. Thanks to the parameters' flexibility, our algorithm can reduce to a number of existing inertial gradient algorithms with Hessian damping and dry friction. By exploiting variational properties of the Moreau envelope, the proposed algorithm is adapted to address weakly convex nonsmooth optimization problems. In particular, we extend the result on KL exponent for the Moreau envelope of a convex KL function to a broad class of KL functions that are not necessarily convex nor continuous. Simulation results illustrate the efficiency of our algorithm and demonstrate the potential advantages of combining dry-like friction with extrapolation and line search techniques.

</details>


### [114] [Curvature Residual Geometry in Bregman Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05680)
*Vu Khac Ky*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过Bregman损失函数（逆Bregman回归）拟合线性回归模型时的优化几何特性。即使生成势函数φ是强凸的，回归目标函数在θ上仍可能非凸，这造成了生成势函数凸性与拟合模型优化几何之间的鸿沟。作者将Hessian表示为加权Gram矩阵，据此给出了局部强凸性、光滑性以及梯度下降条件线性收敛的简单条件。针对二次-四次势函数，推导了精确的标量凸性条件，识别了负曲率区间，并获得了正曲率的局部和全局充分条件。数值实验表明标量条件在评估点处可能失效而完整Hessian仍保持正定，且随四次项参数增大，梯度下降步长的收敛范围缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在逆Bregman回归中，尽管生成势函数φ是强凸的，但回归目标函数可能是非凸的，这打破了传统凸优化理论的假设。理解这种凸性差距及其对优化算法收敛性的影响，对于理论分析和实际应用都至关重要。

Method: 将目标函数的Hessian表示为加权Gram矩阵，其权重依赖于势函数的导数与当前残差，从而推导局部强凸性、光滑性及梯度下降条件线性收敛的条件。针对二次-四次势函数，通过标量分析推导精确凸性条件、负曲率区间和正曲率充分条件，并辅以数值实验验证。

Result: 获得了逆Bregman回归中Hessian的加权Gram矩阵表示及其局部强凸性、光滑性和梯度下降线性收敛的充分条件。针对二次-四次势函数，给出了精确标量凸性条件与负曲率区间，以及局部和全局正曲率充分条件。数值实验表明标量判据保守（完整Hessian可在标量条件失败时仍正定），且四次项参数增大时梯度下降有效步长范围收窄。

Conclusion: 逆Bregman回归中残差依赖的曲率与生成势函数及设计矩阵相互作用，决定了目标函数的优化几何。即使在强凸势函数下，目标函数也可能非凸，但通过Hessian的加权Gram结构可以刻画局部凸性和收敛性条件，为理解此类模型的优化行为提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We consider linear regression models fitted by minimizing Bregman losses of the form \[ \frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^n \left[ φ(y_i)-φ(x_i^\topθ) -φ'(x_i^\topθ) \bigl(y_i-x_i^\topθ\bigr) \right], \] where \(y_i\) is the observed response and \(x_i^\topθ\) is the linear prediction. Even when the generating potential \(φ\) is strongly convex, the resulting regression objective may be nonconvex in \(θ\). This creates a gap between the convexity of the generating potential and the optimization geometry of the fitted model.
  The Hessian can be written as a weighted Gram matrix whose weights depend on the derivatives of the potential and the current residuals. This representation gives simple conditions for local strong convexity, smoothness, and conditional linear convergence of gradient descent.
  For the quadratic--quartic potential, we derive an exact scalar convexity condition, identify the interval of negative curvature, and obtain local and global sufficient conditions for positive curvature. Numerical experiments indicate that these scalar conditions may fail while the full Hessian remains positive definite at the evaluated points. They also indicate that the range of tested gradient-descent step sizes leading to convergence decreases as the quartic parameter grows.
  These results characterize how residual-dependent curvature interacts with the generating potential and the design matrix in reverse Bregman regression.

</details>


### [115] [A Damped Subspace Splitting Algorithm for Constrained Density Functional Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05682)
*Yuanming Su,Yukuan Hu,Xin Liu,Guanghui Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对约束密度泛函理论（CDFT）计算中的离散优化问题，提出了一种新的子空间分裂重构方法（DASSP），通过解耦Stiefel流形约束与额外非凸二次约束，设计了首个具有严格收敛保证的单环阻尼交替方向乘子法，并在合成及实际CDFT问题上验证了其高可行性精度与高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CDFT中的离散优化问题同时包含Stiefel流形约束和额外的非凸二次约束，现有算法要么无法高精度满足二次约束，要么因双环迭代结构面临收敛困难，亟需一种兼具精度与收敛保证的高效算法。

Method: 首先利用旋转不变性并引入非线性子空间对齐约束，推导出子空间分裂（subspace-splitting）重构以解耦两组约束；基于此提出单环阻尼交替方向乘子法（DASSP），每轮迭代包含谱最小化步、投影梯度步和阻尼对偶上升步三个步骤，均支持高效实现。

Result: 在合成与实际CDFT问题的数值实验中，DASSP达到了高可行性精度，展现出良好的计算效率且不损失鲁棒性；该方法为CDFT领域首个具有严格收敛保证的算法。

Conclusion: DASSP为CDFT计算提供了一种可靠、高效且具有收敛保证的优化框架，有望推动大规模CDFT应用的可靠发展。

Abstract: Constrained density functional theory (CDFT) provides a powerful framework for describing electronically excited and charge-localized states, which underlie a broad range of physical and chemical phenomena. However, the discretized optimization problems arising from CDFT calculations remain challenging, owing to the presence of both the Stiefel manifold constraint and additional nonconvex quadratic constraints. Existing algorithms either fail to enforce the quadratic constraints with high accuracy or face convergence issues due to double-loop iterative structures. In this paper, we first derive a subspace-splitting reformulation that decouples the two groups of constraints, by exploiting the inherent rotation invariance and introducing a nonlinear subspace alignment constraint. Based on this reformulation, we propose a single-loop damped alternating direction method of multipliers, called DASSP. To the best of our knowledge, DASSP is the first algorithm for CDFT calculations with rigorous convergence guarantees. Each iteration of DASSP comprises a spectral minimization step, a projected gradient step, and a damped dual ascent step, all of which admit efficient implementations. Numerical results on synthetic and realistic CDFT problems demonstrate that DASSP attains high feasibility accuracy and exhibits favorable efficiency without compromising robustness. We expect that this work will pave the way toward reliable and efficient large-scale CDFT applications.

</details>


### [116] [Quadratic Degree Sequence Optimization and the Critical Roots of a Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05827)
*Frédéric Meunier,Shmuel Onn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究图的度序列优化问题，即给定一个图，寻找其子图使得所有顶点上某个函数的和最大化。文章重点研究二次函数情况下的复杂度，并引入图的"临界根"概念，证明这些根定义的区间上，最优价值函数关于二次根是凸的分段仿射函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度序列优化问题是一个基础且重要的组合优化问题，但对其二次函数情况下的复杂度刻画尚不清楚。作者希望理解该问题在不同参数（二次根）下的结构性质，从而揭示其计算复杂度的本质。

Method: 作者引入了"临界根"（critical roots）这一新概念来刻画图的结构特性，并利用它们将二次根轴划分为若干区间，在每个区间上分析最优解的结构，进而证明最优价值函数在这些区间上具有凸分段仿射性质。

Result: 证明了图的临界根定义了若干区间，在这些区间上，度序列优化问题的最优值作为二次根的函数是凸的分段仿射函数。

Conclusion: 通过临界根对最优价值函数的结构性刻画，为理解二次度序列优化问题的复杂度提供了理论基础，为进一步设计算法或证明难解性奠定了基础。

Abstract: The degree sequence optimization problem is to find a subgraph of a given graph which maximizes the sum over all vertices of a given function evaluated at the subgraph degree of that vertex. Here we study this problem and its complexity for quadratic functions. In particular, we introduce the critical roots of a graph, and show they define intervals over which the optimal value of the problem, as the quadratic root varies, is convex piecewise affine.

</details>


### [117] [Robust priority-aware coverage optimization for aerial sensor networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05873)
*Vanshika Datta,C. Nahak,J. C. Yao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向空中传感器网络的优先级感知鲁棒覆盖优化框架，在传感器位置不确定条件下最大化加权覆盖，并通过PAROO算法实现高效求解，在机场监控场景中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中传感器网络在实际部署中面临传感器位置不确定性，且不同监控区域具有差异化的重要程度（优先级），现有方法未能同时兼顾鲁棒性与优先级加权覆盖的优化需求。

Method: 构建数学优化模型，融合监控约束与基于RRF（鲁棒性表述）的鲁棒性构建；提出优先级感知鲁棒方向优化（PAROO）算法，通过确定传感器方向来最大化加权覆盖目标。

Result: 在机场监控场景中，所提框架能将感知资源有效导向高优先级区域，相比代表性基线方法获得更高的加权覆盖，验证了其在安全敏感环境中的实际适用性。

Conclusion: 该框架在安全敏感场景（如机场监控）中兼具鲁棒性和优先级感知能力，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: This article presents a priority-aware robust coverage optimization framework for an aerial sensor network under sensor location uncertainty. Each region is assigned a priority weight, and the objective is to maximize the weighted coverage while maintaining robustness against positional perturbations. A mathematical optimization model is developed by incorporating surveillance constraints and an RRF-based robustness formulation into the proposed framework. An efficient priority-aware robust orientation optimization (PAROO) algorithm is then proposed to determine the sensor orientations that maximize the weighted coverage objective. Experimental results on an airport-inspired surveillance scenario demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively directs sensing resources toward high-priority regions and achieves higher weighted coverage than representative baseline approaches, highlighting its practical applicability in security-sensitive environments.

</details>


### [118] [Exact Anchoring and a Dualization-Based Matheuristic for Bi-Level Dual-Defense Network Interdiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05922)
*Wei-Chang Yeh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为双层防御攻击者模型（BDAM）提供了精确最优解，该模型耦合节点阻断、边破坏和容量约束的供应支持。研究表明BDAM可通过下层对偶化实现精确单层重构，在18个基准配置上均能证明最优性（17个在10秒内），并揭示了供应规则在平局情况下的不适定性，同时提出MILP-DA方法在多数配置上优于原有混合元启发式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层阻断模型通常依靠元启发式求解，但无法评估解的质量，因为在大规模测试中缺少精确最优解作为参照。

Method: 1) 通过对下层进行对偶化，将BDAM的主导攻击者路径项重构为精确单层混合整数规划（MILP）；2) 提出威胁走廊公式化以解决供应规则在平局条件下不适定的问题；3) 设计MILP-DA算法，将精确锚定与走廊解码及可行性修复相结合。

Result: 精确MILP在全部18个三行和五行基准配置上证明了最优性，其中17个在10秒内完成，并在15×30网格上获得强未经认证的可行解。原有的元启发式平均低于精确前沿3.55%，且差距随规模增大。MILP-DA在18个认证配置中的13个和全部18个更大配置上优于已发表结果，所有失败均出现在三行网格上。平局处理使目标函数移动小于10^(-3)，但实际供应成本最多变化0.83单位。

Conclusion: 通过精确单层重构可以为BDAM提供认证的最优解，证明了先前元启发式方法存在系统性偏差；平局缺陷被目标权重抑制而非真正消除；MILP-DA在大多数场景中优于已有方法。

Abstract: Bi-level interdiction models are frequently solved by metaheuristics whose solution quality cannot be assessed, because exact optima are unavailable at the scales tested. We supply them for the bi-level dual-defense attacker model (BDAM), which couples node interdiction, edge destruction and capacitated supply support, and which we previously solved by a hybrid metaheuristic. First, BDAM's dominant attacker-path term admits an exact single-level reformulation by lower-level dualization, a reduction available whenever arc lengths are linear in the defender's binary decisions; the resulting mixed-integer program certifies optimality on all eighteen three-row and five-row configurations of our earlier benchmark, seventeen in under ten seconds, with strong uncertified incumbents out to 15*30 grids. Our published averages sit 3.55% below that frontier, and the gap widens with scale. Second, the supply rule, like any rule priced on a single attacker shortest path, is ill-posed under ties, and our threat-corridor formulation is tie-invariant by construction. The tie-break moves the objective by under 10^(-3) but the realized supply cost by up to 0.83 units, so the defect is suppressed by the objective weight rather than absent. Third, MILP-DA pairs the exact anchor with the corridor decode and a feasibility repair; compared against our published figures with no re-implementation on either side, it wins on thirteen of eighteen certified configurations and on all eighteen larger ones, every loss falling on a three-row grid.

</details>


### [119] [Stabilizer Design for Policy Iteration in Stochastic Linear Quadratic Control: A Spectrum-Assignment Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05953)
*Xinyu Cao,Bing-Chang Wang,Ying Cao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对带有乘性噪声的连续时间随机系统，提出了一种基于谱配置的模型无关策略迭代初始化方法，无需初始稳定控制器即可实现最优控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略迭代（PI）在模型无关场景下的初始化阶段通常需要完整的模型信息来寻找初始稳定控制器，这对于带有乘性噪声的随机系统尤为困难——因为这类系统的稳定性由包含漂移项和扩散项的Lyapunov型不等式决定，而非确定性系统中的Hurwitz条件。因此需要一种不依赖完整模型信息的初始化方法。

Method: 提出一种新颖的谱配置方法：利用Lyapunov型算子的谱特性，通过调节累积因子逐步从稳定的辅助系统逼近原始系统，从而获得稳定化控制增益。随后，利用系统数据并调整累积因子，设计了一个不依赖初始稳定策略的模型无关算法来实现最优控制。

Result: 通过仿真结果验证了所提方法的有效性，表明该方法能够为连续时间不定随机线性二次控制中的策略迭代提供初始稳定器，并实现模型无关的最优控制。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了带有乘性噪声的随机系统中模型无关策略迭代的初始化难题，通过谱配置方法消除了对初始稳定控制器的依赖，扩展了策略迭代在模型无关场景下的适用范围。

Abstract: Policy iteration (PI) is an important reinforcement learning tool for solving optimal control problems which includes an initialization stage, i.e., the search for an initial stabilizing controller. However, the initialization stage typically relies on complete model information, thereby imposing substantial constraints on the initialization of model-free PI. For stochastic systems with multiplicative noise dependent on state and control, the stability is not ensured by Hurwitz conditions as in the deterministic case, but rather by a Lyapunov-type inequality that incorporates both drift and diffusion terms. Therefore, the corresponding model-free PI initialization problem is more challenging. To this end, a novel spectrum assignment method is proposed to obtain an initial stabilizer for PI in continuous-time indefinite stochastic linear quadratic control. With the help of the Lyapunov-type operator's spectrum, the original system is gradually approximated from the stable auxiliary system by adjusting a cumulative factor, thereby obtaining a stabilizing control gain. Furthermore, by leveraging system data and adjusting the cumulative factor, we design a model-free algorithm that does not rely on an initial stabilizing policy and can achieve optimal control. Finally, simulation results are provided to validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [120] [Distributed Fault Diagnosis in Discrete Event Systems with Transmission Delay Impairments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06035)
*Jiwei Wang,Simone Baldi,Wenwu Yu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了部分观测离散事件系统中的分布式故障诊断问题，提出了一种考虑传输延迟影响的分布式可诊断性条件及其验证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式诊断方法未考虑传输延迟对诊断决策的影响，而在实际系统中延迟不可避免，因此需要一种能够处理延迟场景下的分布式可诊断性验证方法。

Method: 提出了一种新的分布式可诊断性条件，该条件扩展了文献中已有的分散式可诊断性条件；并设计了一种名为"延迟记录器"的新型结构以及一个新的诊断函数来验证该条件。

Result: 理论分析表明所提出的验证方法能够成功判断故障是否可被诊断。

Conclusion: 该工作为存在传输延迟的部分观测离散事件系统提供了分布式可诊断性的判定方法，填补了相关领域的空白。

Abstract: This note studies the distributed fault diagnosis problem in partially-observed discrete event systems, where the system is monitored by a group of agents to cooperatively diagnose faults within a finite number of steps. The novelty of this work is the creation of a methodology to verify when the faults can be diagnosed even in the presence of transmission delay impairments. To address this scenario, a new distributed diagnosability condition is proposed, which extends decentralized diagnosability conditions proposed in the literature. Such distributed diagnosability condition is then verified via a novel structure named delay recorder and a new diagnosis function. Theoretical analysis shows that the verification method can successfully determine whether the faults can be diagnosed.

</details>


### [121] [New Lower Bounds for Weak Limited Augmented Zarankiewicz Numbers in the $m\times 3$ Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06050)
*Liqun Qi,Yannan Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文精确确定了弱限制增广Zarankiewicz数 z_{wL}(m,3) 在 m=5 到 9 时的值，均为 2m，并通过显式构造与有限搜索证明，同时由此推导出 BSR(m,3) ≥ 2m。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在精确计算弱限制增广Zarankiewicz数 z_{wL}(m,3) 在小参数情形下的确切值，以验证并支持关于 z_{wL}(m,3)=2m 对所有 m≥5 成立的猜想，同时为广播即兴冗余（BSR）问题提供下界依据。

Method: 首先通过显式弱容许构造给出下界；对 m=7,8,9 的情形，通过对无 C4 的规范基图进行精确有限搜索来获得匹配的上界；结合已知的小参数情形归纳验证猜想。

Result: 精确确定 z_{wL}(5,3)=10, z_{wL}(6,3)=12, z_{wL}(7,3)=14, z_{wL}(8,3)=16, z_{wL}(9,3)=18，即对 5≤m≤9 均有 z_{wL}(m,3)=2m；由此推出 BSR(m,3)≥2m。

Conclusion: 这些结果有力地支持了 z_{wL}(m,3)=2m 对所有 m≥5 成立的猜想，并为广播即兴冗余问题提供了新的下界。

Abstract: We determine the exact weak limited augmented Zarankiewicz numbers $z_{wL}(m,3)$ for $m=5,6,7,8,9$. We establish \[ z_{wL}(5,3)=10,\quad z_{wL}(6,3)=12,\quad z_{wL}(7,3)=14,\quad z_{wL}(8,3)=16,\quad z_{wL}(9,3)=18. \] Thus $z_{wL}(m,3)=2m$ for all $5\le m\le 9$. The lower bounds are given by explicit weak admissible constructions, while the matching upper bounds for $m=7,8,9$ are obtained by exact finite searches over canonical $C_4$-free base graphs. Together with the known small cases, this strongly supports the conjecture that \[ z_{wL}(m,3)=2m \quad \text{for all } m\ge 5. \] Since BSR$(m,n)\ge z_{wL}(m,n)$, these constructions imply \[ {\rm BSR}(m,3)\ge 2m \quad \text{for } m=5,6,7,8,9. \]

</details>


### [122] [On Same-Sample and Independent-Sample Stochastic Extragradient for Monotone Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06182)
*TaeHo Yoon,Nicolas Loizou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机外梯度（SEG）方法在单调变分不等式问题（VIPs）上的收敛性表现，重点对比了同样本SEG（S-SEG）与独立样本SEG（I-SEG）的差异，并揭示了S-SEG在温和假设下可能不收敛、以及现有步长选择策略失效等关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管外梯度算法在确定性情形下的收敛理论已很成熟，但随机版本的理解仍不充分。现有研究主要集中于独立样本SEG（I-SEG）且假设域紧致或随机算子方差有界；而同样本SEG（S-SEG）——一种性质本质上不同的自然变体——受到的关注甚少。本文旨在填补这一空白，深入理解S-SEG的行为特征。

Method: 本文通过理论分析综合考察了SEG两类变体（I-SEG与S-SEG）的收敛性质。首先在紧致集上构造反例，证明仅凭均值Lipschitz性和有界方差不足以确保S-SEG收敛；随后在可能无界域上，在放宽的假设条件下建立了每个SEG变体的高概率受限区间收敛（restricted-gap convergence），并论证了这些结果的根本改进在一般情况下不可能实现；最后通过构造一个随机单调VIP实例，证明已知的不对称双步长选择对I-SEG可保证几乎必然最后迭代收敛，但对S-SEG会几乎必然发散。

Result: 主要结果包括：（1）S-SEG对样本级Lipschitz参数敏感，均值Lipschitz性和有界方差不足以保证其在紧致集上收敛；（2）在无界域上，为每个SEG变体建立了在放宽假设下的高概率受限区间收敛界，并证明这些结果在一般情况下不可实质改进；（3）构造了随机单调VIP反例，表明对I-SEG有效的不对称双步长策略在S-SEG上会导致几乎必然发散。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了S-SEG与I-SEG在收敛性质上存在根本性差异：S-SEG对样本级光滑性参数高度敏感，且某些对I-SEG有效的经典策略（如不对称双步长）在S-SEG上会失效甚至导致发散。这些发现表明，在实际应用中若采用同样本采样策略，需要比I-SEG更强的假设条件才能保证收敛。

Abstract: We study stochastic extragradient (SEG) methods for solving monotone variational inequality problems (VIPs) over a feasible set. Although extragradient is a foundational algorithm for VIPs and its deterministic convergence theory is well developed, its stochastic counterpart remains less understood. Most existing analyses focus on independent-sample SEG (I-SEG) and assume either that the domain is compact or that the variance of the stochastic operator is uniformly bounded. The behavior of same-sample SEG (S-SEG), a natural variant with materially different properties, has received far less attention. In this work, we address these gaps in the literature. We first show that S-SEG is sensitive to samplewise Lipschitz parameters: mean Lipschitzness and bounded variance alone do not ensure convergence, even on a compact set. Then, for possibly unbounded domains, we establish a high-probability restricted-gap convergence for each SEG variant under a relaxed set of assumptions, and show that certain fundamental improvements to these results are impossible in general. Finally, we show that a known asymmetric double step-size selection that guarantees almost sure last-iterate convergence for I-SEG can fail for S-SEG: there exists a stochastic monotone VIP for which S-SEG diverges almost surely even under the modified step-sizes.

</details>


### [123] [Characteristic Sensitivity Ensembles for Inference of Hidden Dynamics from Marginal Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06190)
*Qi Wang,Gustaaf Jacobs*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于广义常微分方程组动力学推断的框架，通过随机梯度方法从观测到的边缘概率密度函数推断动力学。该方法将扩散及其他不可逆过程重新表述为增广状态空间中的确定性可逆流，利用特征线表示求解Liouville方程，并通过交叉U统计量构造无偏梯度估计器，实现随机梯度下降。四个实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从部分可观测的低维状态中推断不可逆或随机动力学的参数是一个具有挑战性的逆问题。现有方法通常需要求解高维Liouville方程，计算代价高昂。本文的核心动机是将不可逆/随机动力学重新表述为增广状态空间中的确定性哈密顿流，从而将推断问题转化为从边缘观测恢复确定性ODE的问题，降低推断难度。

Method: 1) 将随机/不可逆过程重构为增广状态空间中的确定性可逆流，其中联合密度满足双曲型Liouville方程；2) 观测到的边缘分布是双曲动力学在观测坐标上的投影，潜在分量承载随机性和记忆；3) 算法利用Liouville方程的特征线表示而非直接求解高维方程，初始粒子沿特征线传输；4) 通过沿特征线的灵敏度传播获得关于参数的欧拉灵敏度，并使用交叉U统计量构造无偏梯度估计器；5) 该无偏梯度估计器支持随机梯度下降优化。

Result: 通过四个实验验证了方法：1) 三模态线性系统（通过单模态边缘观测恢复）；2) 含隐藏模态的非线性Gompertz增长模型；3) 双稳态系统（隐藏模态使单峰边缘分布变为双峰）；4) 细胞流中粒子的Stokes-Oseen阻力定律恢复。所有实验均报告了收敛行为的分析。

Conclusion: 该框架成功地将不可逆或随机动力学的参数推断转化为确定性ODE的恢复问题，避免了高维Liouville方程的直接求解。通过特征线方法和无偏梯度估计的随机梯度下降，方法在多种动力学系统上展现了良好的推断能力和收敛性能，为复杂随机动力系统的参数识别提供了新的计算途径。

Abstract: A framework is developed for the inference of dynamics described by a generalized system of ordinary differential equations. A stochastic gradient method is coined that infers dynamics from observed marginal probability density functions using the joint probability density function of the observable and latent variables. Diffusion and other irreversible processes observed in a low-dimensional state can be recast as deterministic, reversible flows in a sufficiently augmented state space, where the joint density satisfies the hyperbolic Liouville equation. The marginal distribution observed is the projection of these hyperbolic dynamics onto the observed coordinates, with the latent components carrying the randomness and memory. This reframing allows inference for irreversible or stochastic dynamics into the recovery of a deterministic Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) from marginal observations. Instead of solving the high-dimensional Liouville equation for the joint density, the algorithm exploits its characteristic representation. Particles sampled from the initial distribution are transported along characteristic lines. The Eulerian sensitivity with respect to parameters is obtained by sensitivity propagation along the characteristic lines, with a crossed U-statistic producing an unbiased gradient estimator, which enables stochastic gradient descent. Four experiments validate the method: recovery of a three-mode linear system observed through the marginal of a single mode; a nonlinear Gompertz growth model with a hidden mode; a bistable system whose hidden mode turns a unimodal marginal bimodal; and Stokes--Oseen drag law recovery for particles in a cellular flow. Convergence behavior is analyzed across these settings.

</details>


### [124] [Muon on the Stiefel Manifold Admits an Exact Closed-Form Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06218)
*Mikhail Solonko,Molozhavenko Alexander,Maxim Rakhuba*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究Muon优化方法在Stiefel流形（正交列矩阵流形）上的扩展，发现该更新存在精确闭式解，并据此提出Skewon算法，同时给出其在光滑非凸条件下的收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon是一种矩阵感知优化方法，但其在Stiefel流形上的现有扩展依赖于启发式、近似或迭代更新，计算效率不一。需要一个精确且高效的算法来处理正交约束优化问题。

Method: 证明了Stiefel流形上的Muon更新存在精确闭式解，基于此闭式解设计Skewon算法，并提供了高效实现。

Result: 提出Skewon算法，具有高效实现，并建立了在光滑非凸设置下的一阶收敛保证。

Conclusion: Skewon为Stiefel流形上的正交约束优化提供了精确、高效且有理论保证的Muon扩展方案。

Abstract: We study Muon, a recently proposed matrix-aware optimization method, in the context of the Stiefel manifold. This manifold consists of matrices with orthonormal columns and is ubiquitous in machine learning and scientific computing. Existing extensions of Muon to this manifold rely on heuristic, approximate, or iterative updates with varying computational efficiency. We show that the corresponding Stiefel Muon update admits an exact closed-form solution and use this result to develop Skewon, a practical algorithm for orthogonality-constrained optimization with an efficient implementation. We further establish first-order convergence guarantees for Skewon in the smooth non-convex setting.

</details>


### [125] [Localized Stabilization of Transport PDEs by Interior Flux Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06249)
*Constantinos Kitsos,Ian Manchester*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究多维连续性方程在有限域上的局部内部通量反馈镇定问题，通过设计散度形式的反馈使误差满足带局部阻尼的输运方程，在有限时间特征阻尼不等式等几何条件下实现L2指数稳定，并扩展至非线性速度场及ISS估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对多维连续性方程（如守恒律/输运方程）在有限域上的镇定问题，现有方法缺乏对源项和局部通量反馈的充分处理。本文旨在通过局部内部通量反馈实现指数稳定，并建立几何条件、增益条件与稳定性的严格关联。

Method: 1) 将反馈设计为通量散度的函数，使误差满足带局部阻尼的输运方程；2) 提出有限时间特征阻尼不等式作为主要几何条件，要求特征线在有限时间窗口内积累均匀阻尼；3) 建立增益条件（局部阻尼对抗压缩放大）；4) 利用Lyapunov型入口条件保证支持受限轨迹族上的特征阻尼；5) 构造加权Lyapunov泛函给出微分Lyapunov准则及ISS估计；6) 讨论椭圆右逆实现反馈通量，并扩展到非线性状态依赖速度场的情形。

Result: 在增益条件与几何条件（有限时间特征阻尼不等式）下，获得了误差的L2指数稳定性；给出了加权Lyapunov泛函下的微分Lyapunov判据和对加性扰动的输入-状态稳定性（ISS）估计；通过二维数值例子验证了几何、增益和实现条件。

Conclusion: 本文系统建立了多维连续性方程通过局部通量反馈进行指数镇定的理论框架，涵盖了线性与非线性速度场、几何条件刻画、Lyapunov判据、ISS估计及反馈通量的实际实现，为相关控制问题提供了可操作的充分条件。

Abstract: We study stabilization of multidimensional continu- ity equations with source terms on bounded domains by means of localized interior flux feedback. The feedback is prescribed through the divergence of the flux and is chosen so that the error with respect to a reference profile satisfies a transport equation with localized damping. The main geometric condition is a finite-time characteristic damping inequality, requiring relevant characteristics to accumulate a uniform amount of damping over a time horizon. This condition is shown to yield exponential stability in L2 of the error, under a gain condition relating localized damping to compressive amplification of the transport field. Lyapunov-type entrance conditions ensure characteris- tic damping on support-restricted families of trajectories. A weighted Lyapunov functional provides a differential Lyapunov criterion and an input-to-state (ISS) estimate with respect to additive perturbations. We also discuss elliptic right-inverse realizations of the feedback flux and extend the characteristic damping argument to velocity fields depending nonlinearly on the state. A two-dimensional example finally illustrates the geometric, gain, and realization conditions.

</details>


### [126] [Fault Diagnosis and Prognosis in Partially-Observed Discrete Event Systems with Delayed Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06295)
*Jiwei Wang,Simone Baldi,Wenwu Yu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了离散事件系统中观测延迟场景下的故障诊断与预测问题，提出了考虑延迟观测的诊断性和可预测性条件，并引入延迟观测器与新的验证函数来验证这些条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际系统中，观测信息可能存在延迟接收的情况，而现有的故障诊断与预测理论大多假设观测是即时可用的。传统的可预测性概念在延迟观测场景下过于保守，需要修订以避免过度保守性。

Method: 提出两种针对延迟观测的诊断性和可预测性条件，对经典的可预测性概念进行修正以避免保守性，并引入延迟观测器（delay observer）和一种新的验证函数来对这些条件进行验证。

Result: 理论分析表明所提出的验证方法对于系统中的故障诊断与预测是有效的。

Conclusion: 该研究为延迟观测场景下的故障诊断与预测提供了理论框架和有效的验证方法，修正了传统可预测性概念的保守性局限。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis and prognosis in discrete event systems are studied in the scenario where the observations are possibly received with delay. To address this scenario, two conditions for diagnosis and prognosis with delayed observations are proposed, where we show that the state-of-the-art notion of prognosability must be revised to avoid conservativeness. Diagnosability and prognosability conditions are then verified by introducing a delay observer and a new verification function. Theoretical analysis indicates the effectiveness of the verification method for fault diagnosis and prognosis in the system.

</details>


### [127] [The Benefits of an Integrated Approach for Stability-Constrained Power System Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06349)
*Gereon Recht,Benedikt Jahn,Oussama Alaya,Karl-Kiên Cao,Hendrik Lens*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了将稳定措施（惯量支撑和电压稳定）与发输储系统扩展规划同时考虑的集成式电力系统规划方法，发现与传统的顺序规划方法相比，集成方法可降低系统成本，且电网形成型电池储能系统具备双重用途优势，优于STATCOM等单一稳定措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着逆变器型资源（IBR）渗透率不断提高，需要通过替代同步发电机的贡献来维持动态电压稳定性和惯量响应。然而，电力系统规划领域现有的成熟方法多为顺序式规划，即在规划后期才考虑稳定措施，这可能导致规划结果次优。因此需要探索将稳定措施与系统扩展规划同时进行的集成式方法。

Method: 采用集成式电力系统规划方法，将稳定措施（如惯量支撑和电压稳定约束）以简化的稳定约束形式（惯性约束和电压稳定约束）嵌入发输电与储能系统扩展规划模型中，使稳定措施与系统扩展在单一优化框架内同时考虑，并将该方法与传统的顺序规划方法进行对比分析。

Result: 研究表明，采用集成式规划方法可降低系统总成本。同时，相较于静止同步补偿器（STATCOM）等专用稳定措施，电网形成型电池储能系统因其既能提供稳定支撑又具备储能功能（双重用途）而更受青睐。

Conclusion: 集成式电力系统规划方法在同时考虑稳定措施与系统扩展时能够实现更优的经济性，且电网形成型电池储能系统作为兼具稳定支撑与储能的灵活资源，在集成规划框架下展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Increasing penetration of inverter-based resources in today's power systems requires substitution of the contribution from synchronous generators to dynamic voltage stability and inertial response. However, established approaches for power system planning are sequential, as stabilising measures are only considered at a later stage of the planning process. We investigate the advantages of an integrated approach for power system planning, where stabilising measures are considered simultaneously with the expansion of generation, transmission, and storage systems via simplified stability constraints on inertia and voltage stability. We find that system costs are reduced with the integrated approach and that the dual-use option of grid-forming battery energy storage systems is favoured over other stabilising measures like static synchronous compensators.

</details>

### [128] [Quality Diversity for Reliable Data Driven Time-Use Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05230)
*Aneta Neumann,Ty Stanford,Dorothea Dumuid,Frank Neumann*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将预测不确定性嵌入质量多样性（QD）优化的框架，用于生成更可靠的每日时间使用配置推荐。基于成分数据分析（n>1000的儿童队列数据）构建目标函数，同时考虑健康收益期望与模型置信度，在低不确定性区域中发现多样化且高质量的时间使用组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常24小时时间预算的分配与身心健康及认知功能密切相关。现有预测模型虽能估计时间使用组成与健康指标的关系，但多数优化方法仅最大化期望收益，忽略了数据驱动预测中固有的不确定性，这可能导致不切实际的时间使用推荐。

Method: 首先使用成分数据分析（CDA）基于大型儿童队列数据集（n>1000）构建时间使用组成与多种健康指标之间的目标函数；然后开发一种将预测不确定性纳入质量多样性（QD）优化过程的新方法，通过变量式和行为式两种行为表征探索解空间，在期望健康收益与模型置信度之间取得平衡。

Result: 框架能够发现多样化且高质量的时间使用组成，揭示不确定性下健康结果之间的显式关系。通过将不确定性直接嵌入优化，推荐结果向低不确定性区域偏移，同时保留高质量结构，从而实现更可靠的决策支持。

Conclusion: 该不确定性感知的QD框架为行为健康领域的时间使用推荐提供了更可靠的决策工具，将优化从单纯追求期望收益提升到兼顾预测置信度的层面，为数据驱动的健康行为干预提供了新范式。

Abstract: The daily allocation of the finite 24-hour time budget is strongly associated with physical, mental, and cognitive health. While predictive models can estimate the relationship between time-use compositions and health outcomes such as body mass index, life satisfaction, and cognition, most optimization approaches focus only on maximizing expected benefit and do not consider the uncertainty inherent in data-driven prediction. Ignoring uncertainty in health-related decisions can lead to unrealistic time-use recommendations. To address this gap, we introduce an uncertainty quantification Quality Diversity (QD) framework for a more reliable time-use recommendation. Objective functions are derived using compositional data analysis using a large child cohort dataset n > 1000, to capture the relationship between daily activity compositions and multiple health indicators. We develop a new approach that incorporates predictive uncertainty into QD processes and produces more reliable recommendations that balance the expected health benefits with the confidence of the model. We explore the solution space through variable-based and objective-based behavioral representations, revealing diverse high-quality time-use composition and explicit relationships between health outcomes under uncertainty. By embedding uncertainty directly into optimization, our framework shifts the time-use recommendations toward regions of lower uncertainty while preserving high-quality structures for more reliable decision-making in behavioral health.

</details>


### [129] [A Unified Causal Inference Framework for the Desirability of Outcome Ranking Paradigm in Benefit-Risk Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05244)
*Yuan Feng,Shiyu Shu,Yixin Fang,Ionut Bebu,Toshimitsu Hamasaki,Scott Evans,Guoqing Diao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本论文提出一个统一的协变量校正因果推断框架，用于估计随机试验和观察性研究中的"结局排序合意性"（DOOR）概率，以实现获益-风险评估。该框架将DOOR概率表达为边际有序结局分布的二元泛函，通过序贯风险集风险估计条件有序分布，并推导了DOOR概率的有效影响函数（EIF）。模拟研究显示TMLE-SL和CVTMLE-SL在点估计和推断性能上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在临床获益-风险评估中，DOOR概率是一个直观的评估指标，但现有的协变量调整方法在处理该指标时缺乏统一的因果推断框架，尤其是在观察性研究中存在混杂偏倚。作者旨在填补这一方法论空白，为DOOR概率提供稳健且高效的估计与推断工具。

Method: 该框架的核心方法包括：(1) 将DOOR概率表达为两种治疗策略下边际有序结局分布的二元泛函；(2) 通过序贯风险集风险（sequential risk-set hazards）估计条件有序分布；(3) 推导DOOR概率的有效影响函数（EIF）；(4) 在点估计中比较G-computation、归一化逆概率加权（IPW）、增强IPW（AIPW）和靶向最大似然估计（TMLE），其中 nuisance 函数使用广义线性模型或 Super Learner（SL）估计；(5) 在推断中评估AIPW-SL和TMLE-SL（含与不含交叉拟合）的表现。

Result: 模拟结果显示：TMLE-SL在点估计方面表现最强且最稳定，AIPW-SL位居第二。在EIF推断评估中，交叉拟合的CVTMLE-SL在DOOR尺度偏差、有序分布恢复、标准误准确性和置信区间覆盖率方面综合表现最佳。

Conclusion: 提出的统一框架为DOOR概率提供了基于EIF的因果推断方法，其中CVTMLE-SL被推荐作为首选估计策略。该方法通过抗菌药物耐药性领导集团（ARLG）的多重耐药菌网络数据得以实际应用说明。

Abstract: We developed a unified covariate-adjusted causal inference framework for estimating the desirability of outcome ranking (DOOR) probability for benefit-risk evaluation in randomized trials and observational studies. The framework expresses the DOOR probability as a bilinear functional of the marginal ordinal outcome distributions under the two treatment strategies, estimates conditional ordinal distributions through sequential risk-set hazards, and derives the efficient influence function (EIF) of the DOOR probability. The point-estimation simulations compared G-computation, normalized inverse probability weighting (IPW), augmented IPW (AIPW), and targeted maximum likelihood estimation (TMLE), with nuisance functions estimated using generalized linear models or Super Learner (SL). TMLE-SL showed the strongest and most consistent point-estimation performance, with AIPW-SL ranking second. EIF-based inference was then evaluated for AIPW-SL and TMLE-SL, with and without cross-fitting, across settings varying in overlap, treatment-effect heterogeneity, and treatment allocation. CVTMLE-SL showed the strongest overall performance across DOOR-scale bias, recovery of the underlying ordinal distributions, standard-error accuracy, and confidence-interval coverage. We illustrate the methodology using data from the multidrug-resistant organism network of the Antibacterial Resistance Leadership Group.

</details>


### [130] [Deep Generalised Mixed Models: a Novel Neural Network Structure for Analysing Hierarchical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05930)
*Nina van Gerwen,Dimitris Rizopoulos,Manon Hillegers,Loes Keijsers,Sten Willemsen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Deep Generalised Mixed Model（深度广义混合模型）的新型神经网络架构，用于分析经验采样法（ESM）数据。该模型将混合效应模型泛化到深度学习中，能够处理高维、缺失数据（MAR）及非正态纵向结果，并通过变分自编码器和贝叶斯数据增强算法进行估计。在GrowIt!青少年情绪研究中的应用显示其有潜力，但因模型不稳定而表现欠佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验采样法（ESM）数据在COVID-19疫情期间的青少年情绪研究（GrowIt!应用）中产生，现有分析方法面临两大挑战：标准统计技术难以扩展到高维设置，而机器学习方法因缺失数据（MAR类型）引入的选择偏差而产生偏倚结果。

Method: 提出Deep Generalised Mixed Model，该架构将混合效应模型泛化至深度学习框架，通过固定效应和随机效应对数据的均值和相关结构进行半参数灵活建模。估计阶段采用变分自编码器的改编版本和贝叶斯数据增强算法，使模型能够适配遵循一般分布的纵向结果、扩展至高维设置，并在数据缺失随机（MAR）时提供有效推断。

Result: 将Deep Generalised Mixed Model应用于GrowIt!研究及多项模拟实验中，结果显示该模型具有应用潜力，但由于模型不稳定性导致性能次优。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了深度学习与混合效应模型融合用于纵向ESM数据分析的可行性，但仍需进一步改进模型稳定性才能充分发挥其在实际应用中的优势。

Abstract: The experience sampling method (ESM) is a longitudinal research design where participants report their thoughts, emotional states and behaviours multiple times a day. Our work is motivated by such data collected by the GrowIt! app, which was released to investigate daily emotions among adolescents during the COVID-19 pandemic. Current procedures to analyse ESM data face various challenges. While standard statistical techniques may not scale well to a high-dimensional setting, machine learning procedures can give biased results due to selection bias introduced by missingness. In our motivating dataset, adolescents dropped out due to previous strong feelings of negative emotions. Hence, the implied missing data are of the missing-at-random type that standard machine learning procedures cannot accommodate. We develop a novel neural network architecture that generalises mixed effects models to deep learning to overcome these challenges. It allows semi-parametric and flexible modelling of data's mean and correlation structure through fixed and random effects. For estimation, we use an adaptation of variational auto-encoders and a Bayesian data augmentation algorithm. Through this approach, the model can accommodate longitudinal outcomes following generic distributions, scale well to high-dimensional settings and provide valid inference when data are missing-at-random. We applied the Deep Generalised Mixed Model to the GrowIt! study and various simulations. The results show potential for the Deep Generalised Mixed Model, yet suboptimal performance due to model instability.

</details>


### [131] [Handling Missing Data in Probabilistic Regression Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06195)
*Taiane Schaedler Prass,Alisson Silva Neimaier,Guilherme Pumi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文扩展了概率回归树（PRTrees）框架，使其能够直接在树构建过程中处理缺失预测变量，无需预先插补。提出了三种策略（均匀概率、部分观测、降维平滑），并在多个真实数据集上验证了其优于CART的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典回归树（如CART）在处理缺失值时往往需要先进行插补或使用代理分裂准则，而传统插补方法可能引入偏差或丢失信息。PRTrees作为平滑且一致的回归树替代方案，尚未原生支持缺失值处理，这限制了其在实际数据中的应用。

Method: 提出三种在PRTree构建过程中直接处理缺失预测变量的策略：1) 均匀概率方法，将缺失观测均匀分配给所有分裂子节点；2) 部分观测方法，仅利用可用特征计算分裂概率；3) 降维平滑方法，在降低特征维度后进行概率分配。所有策略均保持概率守恒和边际兼容性。

Result: 在多个不同缺失程度的真实数据集上，所提方法与经典回归树进行比较。结果显示缺失观测的处理方式对概率树构建效果影响显著；填充策略是主导建模组件；在大量观测含缺失值的数据集中，所提方法经常优于CART，同时保持树模型的可解释性和灵活性。

Conclusion: 概率回归树通过在构建过程中直接处理缺失值，避免了预插补的需要，并且在缺失比例较高的场景下能够优于经典CART，为缺失数据环境下的树模型预测提供了更优且可解释的方案。

Abstract: Probabilistic Regression Trees (PRTrees) are a smooth and consistent alternative to classical regression trees, producing continuous predictions through probabilistic split assignments. This paper extends the PRTree framework to accommodate missing predictor values directly during tree construction, eliminating the need for prior imputation. Three strategies are proposed, each exploiting the available information differently: a uniform-probability approach, a partial-observation approach, and a dimension-reduced smoothing approach. These modifications are defined to preserve the fundamental probabilistic properties of the original methodology, including probability conservation and marginal compatibility, under arbitrary patterns of missing covariate values. The proposed methods are evaluated on several real-world datasets exhibiting different levels of missingness and are compared with classical regression trees. The results show that the effectiveness of probabilistic tree construction depends strongly on the treatment of missing observations. Across the considered datasets, the fill strategy emerged as the dominant modeling component, often exerting a larger influence on predictive performance than either the smoothing distribution or the proxy-selection criterion. In datasets where a substantial proportion of observations contained missing predictor values, the proposed methods frequently outperformed CART, while maintaining the interpretability and flexibility of tree-based models.

</details>


### [132] [Beyond Marginal Validity: Finite-Sample Guarantees for Localized Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06206)
*Anton Conrad,Rustam Isaev,Denis Belomestny,Eric Moulines,Sergey Samsonov*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文为随机局部化共形预测（RLCP）提供了首个有限样本的高概率保证，在赫尔德正则性等标准假设下，证明其条件覆盖率缺口和长度误差相对于最优预测器（oracle）的界可以分解为局部化偏差与校准项，从而刻画了带宽的偏差-方差权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测的边际有效性可能掩盖特定协变量处的严重校准误差，而精确的分布自由条件覆盖在有限样本下无法实现。RLCP通过在测试点附近校准来弥补这一差距，但现有理论缺乏对实际局部化集合的有限样本保证，无法同时控制条件有效性和最优效率。

Method: 作者在固定得分函数下，利用条件得分CDF的赫尔德正则性、密度函数和核函数的标准假设，证明了对实际局部化邻域一致成立的条件覆盖率缺口和相对于oracle的长度误差的高概率界。这些界分解为O(h^β)的局部化偏差项和随校准样本量减小的校准项。对于数据划分学习的得分函数（如共形分位回归），当得分逼近枢轴得分时，均匀局部保证可分解为固定得分校准误差与均匀得分估计误差。

Result: 推导出条件覆盖率和oracle效率的联合高概率有限样本界，明确刻画了带宽h的偏差-方差权衡，并证明改进的得分学习可以收紧局部化保证，揭示了RLCP在何种条件下能够跟踪oracle性能。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了RLCP在有限样本条件下联合控制条件有效性与效率的理论空白，为理解带宽选择和学习得分对局部化共形预测性能的影响提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Conformal prediction endows arbitrary black-box predictors with finite-sample, distribution-free marginal coverage, yet marginal validity can hide severe covariate-specific miscalibration, while exact distribution-free conditional coverage is finite-sample unattainable. Randomly localized conformal prediction (RLCP) mitigates this gap by calibrating near the test point while preserving marginal coverage. Existing theory, however, lacks finite-sample guarantees for the realized localized set that jointly control conditional validity and oracle efficiency. We provide such guarantees. For any fixed score, under Hölder regularity of the conditional score CDF and standard density and kernel assumptions, we prove high-probability bounds, uniform over a realized localization neighbourhood, for the conditional-coverage gap and the length error relative to the oracle.
